The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [147] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GROW框架，将GRPO从完整轨迹拓展到状态-动作样本级别的相对策略优化，解决多轮强化学习中的长上下文噪声问题，在Minecraft任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM智能体依赖SFT专家演示，高级RL算法GRPO因需要完整轨迹作为训练样本导致上下文过长和噪声，无法有效用于多轮RL任务。

Method: 提出GROW框架，将采集的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本对，在样本间计算优势函数而非整条轨迹；并给出替代分析证明在简化假设下该目标能保留GRPO的核心相对策略优化信号。

Result: 在800多个Minecraft任务上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: GROW框架有效解决了多轮RL中GRPO的应用困境，展示了基于样本级优势计算的RL方法在开放世界VLM智能体中的潜力。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [148] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CP-MoE通过瞬态专家引导的持续学习框架，有效缓解了MoE架构中大语言模型和视觉-语言模型的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法存在根本性权衡：要么过度隔离专家导致跨任务知识迁移受限，要么允许任务特定更新覆盖重要参数导致严重遗忘。

Method: 提出CP-MoE框架，包含三个核心组件：瞬态专家（捕获早期任务特定更新）、一致性保持路由偏置（利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表征相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家）、瞬态专家引导的正则化机制（在合并过程中选择性保护重要历史参数）。

Result: 在SuperNI基准上取得SOTA性能并展现更强的零样本迁移能力；在VQA v2数据集上有效扩展到多模态视觉推理，持续减少遗忘并超越强MoE基线。

Conclusion: CP-MoE通过瞬态专家机制成功平衡了知识迁移与参数保护，为LLM和VLM的持续学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [149] [Graph Transductive Sharpening: Leveraging Unlabeled Predictions in Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20248)
*Brown Zaz,Mar Gonzàlez I Català,Ferran Hernandez Caralt,Moshe Eliasof,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transductive Sharpening（TS），一种针对半监督节点分类的损失级改进方法，通过对未标注节点最小化预测熵、对标注节点平衡熵，在不改变架构的情况下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半监督节点分类方法主要关注架构创新，而忽略了训练目标本身。标准监督目标丢弃了无标签节点的预测信息，作者发现这些预测中包含有用的训练信号。

Method: 受交叉熵分解为标签依赖对齐项和标签无关熵项的启发，提出Transductive Sharpening（TS）：在损失层面，对未标注节点最小化预测熵，同时对标注节点进行平衡处理。

Result: 在多种节点分类基准上取得一致的性能提升，无需改变骨干架构。

Conclusion: 训练目标本身是一个值得探索的重要维度，Transductive Sharpening通过简单有效的损失级修改即可提升半监督节点分类性能。

Abstract: In the transductive setting, where the full graph is observed but node labels are only partially available, progress in semi-supervised node classification has largely focused on architectural innovation. In this paper, we revisit an orthogonal axis: the training objective. We start from a simple observation: transductive models produce predictions for every node during training, including nodes without labels. These unlabeled-node predictions may contain useful training signal, but standard supervised objectives discard them because no ground-truth labels are available. Inspired by the decomposition of cross-entropy into a label-dependent alignment term and a label-independent entropy term, we propose prediction confidence as a natural way to extract this signal in the absence of labels. This motivates Transductive Sharpening (TS): a loss-level modification that minimizes prediction entropy on unlabeled nodes while counterbalancing this effect on labeled nodes. We evaluate Transductive Sharpening across a wide range of node-classification benchmarks and observe consistent performance improvements without requiring any changes to the backbone architecture. Code is available at https://github.com/transductive-sharpening/tunedGNN.

</details>


### [150] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种LLM驱动的进化框架Kernel Discovery，通过两阶段方法（先提出数学形式再转可执行代码）和LOO-CRPS选择准则，在高维贝叶斯优化中自动搜索更广泛的核函数空间，在5个基准上平均排名1.2/17。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯过程核函数设计在高维问题中依赖人工，自动化方法受限于搜索空间小，而LLM方法因上下文长度限制和观测噪声难以处理原始数据。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM驱动进化框架：第一阶段LLM提出新颖数学形式，第二阶段将形式转换为可执行代码；使用留一法连续排名概率评分(LOO-CRPS)作为选择准则以防止过拟合。

Result: 在5个高维贝叶斯优化基准上，平均排名1.2/17，优于所有对比基线。

Conclusion: Kernel Discovery能够自动发现比预定义组合规则更广泛的核函数，有效提升高维BO性能，且无需依赖原始观测数据。

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [151] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的联合训练框架，让自动驾驶汽车与12个行人共同训练，行人的乱穿马路行为由隐藏的性格特质驱动。相比固定策略基线，联合训练的SDC目标完成率达78%（碰撞率14%），而最佳规则基线为35%（碰撞率33%）。乱穿马路仅占13%的过街事件，却导致了62%的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于脚本或简化行人模型的仿真测试无法捕捉真实行人过街行为的异构性和不确定性，尤其是在乱穿马路场景中，行人性格特质对行为的影响被忽略，限制了安全评估的真实性。

Method: 构建MARL环境，使用MAPPO算法联合训练SDC和12个行人。行人底层运动采用Dijkstra路径规划，高层决策（走/等）由RL策略控制。乱穿马路概率由每个行人独立的隐藏性格特质决定，SDC无法观测该特质。

Result: 联合训练SDC目标完成率78%（碰撞率14%），优于规则基线35%（33%碰撞）。SDC在乱穿行人附近比在斑马线行人附近速度快2.65 m/s（0-3m范围内），表明未预判乱穿行为。乱穿占过街事件13%但关联62%碰撞。MARL相比单智能体RL降低30%碰撞。

Conclusion: MARL联合训练能生成更真实的交互场景，行人学会在SDC高速接近时等待，有效降低了碰撞率。乱穿马路是SDC安全测试的关键挑战，隐藏性格特质带来的不确定性对安全评估至关重要。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [152] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FBOS-RL框架，通过反馈引导的探索增强和双目标协同训练（利用性策略对齐EPA + 探索性能力培养ECC），解决GRPO在困难任务上采样质量差导致训练停滞的问题，实现更快的训练速度和更高的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在任务超出当前策略模型能力时，简单采样方案难以产生高质量rollout，导致缺乏有意义的梯度方向，训练陷入停滞。

Method: 提出FBOS-RL框架：1) 基于环境反馈的反馈引导探索增强；2) 两个相互强化的训练目标——利用性策略对齐(EPA)和探索性能力培养(ECC)，形成正反馈飞轮效应。

Result: 在相同rollout数量下，FBOS-RL比GRPO和基于反馈的基线学习更快，最终性能上限更高，且训练过程中保持更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Conclusion: EPA和ECC可以相互强化，形成正反馈飞轮效应，显著提升强化学习的训练效率和最终性能上限。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [153] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于链接表示的自监督学习模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，用于链路预测任务，并通过社区结构的图增强方法提升性能，在无属性图上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习在图节点分类任务上表现良好，但在链路预测任务上的研究较少，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 系统评估现有自监督模型在链路预测上的表现，发现数据增强是关键。2) 提出基于社区结构的图增强方法。3) 提出基于链接表示（而非节点表示）的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL。

Result: L-GRACE和L-BGRL在无属性图上显著优于现有方法，在监督和自监督场景下均达到与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 基于链接表示的自监督学习范式对链路预测任务更有效，社区结构增强是提升性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

</details>


### [154] [Residual Paving: Diagnosing the Routing Bottleneck in Selective Refusal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20262)
*Bryce Hinkley,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Residual Paving，一种基于路由残差编辑的方法，通过解耦路由选择与残差编辑能力，实现对冻结指令微调模型的选择性拒绝编辑，在保持良性行为和有害拒绝的同时，显著降低编辑提示的拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在选择性拒绝编辑上效果不佳，难以同时实现编辑提示的非拒绝、良性行为的保持以及有害拒绝的保留，存在路由选择性瓶颈。

Method: 提出Residual Paving方法，包含早期层路由预测标量门控和专家混合；当激活时，基于提示的瓶颈残差专家在后期层应用残差更新。通过oracle路由诊断（用真实标签替换学习到的门控）来评估路由选择性的瓶颈。

Result: 在Gemma-3-4B-IT上，编辑拒绝率从88.6%降至4.0%，良性分布保持95.5%，有害拒绝保持87.3%。对比方法编辑拒绝率仍高达86.8%和78.9%。oracle路由在六个基座上均提升诊断分数，中位数增益+12.9 pp。

Conclusion: 路由选择性是实现有效选择性拒绝编辑的主要瓶颈；Residual Paving的解耦设计有效；编辑方向更倾向于目标延续而非通用拒绝抑制。

Abstract: We study selective refusal editing as a three-way control problem: induce non-refusal on designated edit prompts while preserving benign behavior and harmful refusals outside the edit set. We introduce Residual Paving, a routed residual editing method for frozen instruction-tuned transformers that separates route selectivity, whether to intervene, from residual-edit capacity, what edit to apply. An early-layer router predicts a scalar gate and expert mixture; when active, prompt-conditioned bottleneck residual experts apply later-layer residual updates while leaving the backbone unchanged. This decomposition supports an oracle-routing diagnostic where only the learned scalar gate is replaced with the held-out edit/keep label, leaving the residual editor and frozen backbone fixed. On the primary Gemma-3-4B-IT held-out split, learned Residual Paving reduces edit refusal from 88.6% to 4.0%, with 95.5% benign distribution preservation and 87.3% harmful distribution preservation. Same-protocol one-direction steering controls are much weaker on edit success, leaving edit refusal at 86.8% for Edit-target ActAdd and 78.9% for DIM-style refusal steering. The remaining failure is off-target harmful-keep degradation: harmful refusal remains below the frozen-base rate, 65.3% vs. 81.6%. Across six backbones, oracle routing improves the keep-side diagnostic score on every reported row, with median gain +12.9 pp, supporting the interpretation that learned route selectivity is the main observed bottleneck. Trajectory diagnostics on two backbones further suggest directed movement toward edit-target continuations rather than generic refusal suppression.

</details>


### [155] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分段平稳低秩线性上下文赌博机，兼顾了奖励的低维子空间结构和子空间漂移问题，提出了SPSC算法，在识别边界、动态遗憾和实证表现三个维度上取得了紧致结果，首次在该设定下达到内在秩的动态遗憾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作要么假设奖励位于低维潜在子空间（低秩赌博机）但无法处理子空间变化，要么能适应漂移（非平稳线性赌博机）却付出环境维度d的代价。实际部署中两方面共存，亟需统一框架。

Method: 提出SPSC算法，交替进行各向同性探测和窗口化投影岭UCB利用，在学习的r维子空间内决策；CUSUM风格变体在线检测分段边界。理论分析给出三项紧致结果：识别边界的充要条件、动态遗憾率（Õ(r√T)+Õ(T^{2/3})）、以及环境维与内在维的交叉点d-r≳T^{1/6}。

Result: 在11个基准（合成、UCI/MovieLens、半合成临床、ZOZOTOWN生产日志）上，SPSC在d-r≳T^{1/6}时超越非平稳和低秩基线，与理论交叉点吻合。

Conclusion: 首次在分段平稳低秩线性上下文赌博机设定下，刻画了子空间可识别性的边界条件，并达到内在秩的动态遗憾率，统一了低秩和非平稳两个方向。

Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

</details>


### [156] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSA（Conformal Selective Acting）方法，作为面向RLVR微调本地专家LLM部署的安全认证框架。CSA通过为每个阈值维护Ville型e-process并结合Bonferroni网格，在可预测更新和单调风险假设下，实现了任意时间路径选择性风险控制，确保每次部署轮次都能提供安全证书，且不依赖长期平均或跨部署池化。实验覆盖48个专家基准、16个对抗分布偏移和5个在线RLVR场景，CSA是唯一在所有测试中满足路径有效性和无拒绝部署的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合规包装器无法满足在线自适应更新的本地专家LLM部署的每轮安全认证需求：离线保形风险方法要求可交换性，在线保形方法只控制长期平均，非可交换扩展仅保证边际有效性，A-RCPS控制边际而非选择性风险。监管机构要求每次部署轮次都提供安全证书，且不能跨部署池化、不能等待长期平均。

Method: CSA采用（检验统计量、有效性保证、部署规则）框架，识别出唯一空缺单元：每阈值e-process、选择性风险、任意时间路径有效性、最大认证阈值规则。具体实现为在Bonferroni网格上为每个阈值维护Ville型e-process，并针对RLVR过滤进行评估。

Result: 理论上证明：①任意时间路径选择性风险界R_T^act ≤ α + O(N_T^{-1/2})；②速率最优认证匹配Θ(η̄^{-2}log(1/δ))；③存在与时间跨度无关的发布率间隙。实验上，在8个专家基准（480个流）、16个对抗分布偏移（160个流）和5个在线RLVR场景（10300轮）中，CSA是唯一在所有测试中满足路径有效性和无拒绝部署的方法。

Conclusion: CSA不是新的LLM、训练算法或策略类，而是部署端的安全认证补充，与模型正交。对于无法使用前沿API的运营商，CSA提供了满足严格监管要求的安全部署保障。通过控制每轮的选择性风险而非边际风险，CSA填补了现有在线保形方法在监管认证场景中的关键空白。

Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

</details>


### [157] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)
*Nasehatul Mustakim,Lucas Lehnert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出RL智能体在POMDP中实现分布外（OOD）泛化的理论模型，通过抽象函数和成功加权模型压缩，证明了在更复杂任务上泛化的条件，并指出限制智能体在小型抽象状态空间上操作是实现泛化的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松地将抽象概念泛化到更复杂或更大的任务中，而现有的强化学习系统缺乏这种能力，因此作者希望从理论上建模和解释RL智能体如何实现分布外泛化。

Method: 将现有状态抽象框架扩展到POMDP；定义成功加权模型缩减（successor-weighted model reduction）以实现更紧的压缩；推导OOD测试性能的边界，将性能损失分解为近似误差和估计误差。

Result: 推导出OOD泛化性能的理论边界，表明减小抽象状态空间规模可以提升测试性能和OOD泛化能力；性能损失可分解为近似误差和估计误差两部分。

Conclusion: 限制智能体在小型、有限的抽象状态集合上操作是实现向更复杂任务泛化的必要条件；该结果鼓励进一步研究跨不同复杂度任务扩展的RL架构。

Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

</details>


### [158] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniISR框架，通过中间监督和正则化信号统一集中学习、联邦学习及混合训练模式，在理论上证明了O(1/√T)收敛界、客户端漂移减少和梯度对齐保证，实验显示CL-FL差距减少22.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同地区对数据隐私的法律框架不同（允许集中学习vs要求数据本地化），导致集中学习和联邦学习两种优化范式互不兼容，缺乏统一的理论保障。

Method: 提出OmniISR框架，在多个隐藏层引入中间监督和正则化信号：(1) 用互信息(MI)对齐集中学习中的内部协变量偏移和联邦学习中的客户端漂移；(2) 用负熵(NE)惩罚过度自信预测、保持表征不确定性、避免设备特定坍缩。

Result: 跨多个联邦学习算法获得37/48配对指标胜利，模型性能一致提升，CL-FL差距减少22.60%。

Conclusion: 提出的ISR信号不违反标准SGD收敛（O(1/√T)非渐近界），能减少联邦客户端漂移，保证CL和FL更新在温和偏差下不冲突，且CL-FL混合能加速逃离严格鞍点。

Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees. To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

</details>


### [159] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，利用基于参考的原子化声明差异作为奖励单元进行长文本图像描述强化学习，解决了传统标量奖励无法区分幻觉与遗漏事实的权衡问题，在多个基准上实现了更平衡的忠实度与覆盖率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本图像描述中的强化学习存在奖励粒度问题：模型以序列整体被评判，但关键错误发生在单个视觉声明层面。传统标量奖励将局部错误压缩为单一信号，混淆了事实性（避免幻觉）与覆盖率（不遗漏重要细节）之间的权衡。

Method: 提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，利用多模态法官枚举演员描述与参考描述之间的原子化声明差异，针对每项差异进行图像验证、分配开放词汇错误类型和严重程度，并生成逐差异统计用于奖励组合，使幻觉声明和遗漏事实可分别测量与调节。

Result: 在160张图像的人工标注诊断基准、公开描述基准和VQA基准上，ClaimDiff-RL改善了幻觉-遗漏事实平衡，保持通用能力，在物体计数、空间关系和场景识别等多个细粒度能力维度上甚至超越Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: 类型化、可验证的声明差异是一种有效的奖励单元，可用于细粒度且可诊断的图像描述强化学习。

Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

</details>


### [160] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出内省训练(IXT)方法，利用后期训练阶段的思维奖励模型生成自然语言反馈，通过前缀条件化数据，使早期训练阶段就能区分不同token质量，实现最高2.8倍的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练流水线包含多个阶段（预训练、后训练等），但各阶段之间存在信息孤岛，后训练阶段的动态信息无法有效指导前期的预训练过程，导致整体训练效率不够高。

Method: 受离线奖励条件强化学习启发，提出IXT方法。使用思维奖励模型对数据标注自然语言评论反馈，训练时将反馈作为前缀条件加入到数据中，使模型从训练早期就能区分不同质量的数据token。

Result: 在7.5B-12B参数规模的稠密Transformer模型上，从头训练至18万亿token，IXT方法使缩放曲线发生弯曲，最高提升2.8倍计算效率；在数学和代码领域达到传统训练方法无法企及的性能水平。

Conclusion: IXT通过跨阶段信息传递实现了更高效的LLM训练缩放，特别是在数学和代码等复杂领域表现出显著优势。

Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

</details>


### [161] [FusionCell: Cross-Attentive Fusion of Layout Geometry and Netlist Topology for Standard-Cell Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20287)
*Haoyi Zhang,Kairong Guo,Bojie Zhang,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionCell是一种双模态预测器，融合了布局几何与网表拓扑，在7nm工艺下实现0.92%的平均MAPE，显著加速标准单元表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准单元表征依赖慢速仿真扫描，现有快速预测器忽略布局几何，无法捕捉耦合和布局依赖效应，需要联合表示布局几何与网表拓扑以实现精确性能预测。

Method: 提出FusionCell双模态模型：DeiT编码器处理三层布线的布局几何，图变压器建模异构器件/网图；通过拓扑引导机制，网表作为结构"地图"主动查询布局相关物理区域，实现联合推理。

Result: 在基于ASAP7 PDK的7nm数据集（超19.5k单元、149种类型）上，平均MAPE为0.92%，Spearman/Kendall排序优于基线，表征速度比电路仿真快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 融合布局几何和网表拓扑的双模态方法能有效提升标准单元性能预测精度与效率，为快速电路表征提供新范式。

Abstract: Standard cells form the building blocks of digital circuits, so their delay and power critically influence chip-level performance; yet characterization still relies on slow simulation sweeps, and many fast predictors ignore layout geometry, missing coupling and layout-dependent effects. The challenge is to jointly represent layout geometry and netlist topology so models capture fine-grained spatial details together with structural connectivity for accurate performance prediction. We introduce FusionCell, a dual-modality predictor that treats routed layout geometry and netlist topology as inputs and fuses them explicitly in a unified model. A DeiT encoder processes three-layer routed layouts, while a graph transformer models heterogeneous device/net graphs. The modalities are integrated through a topology-guided mechanism, where the netlist acts as a structural "map" to actively query relevant physical regions in the layout for joint geometric and topological reasoning. We build a 7nm dataset based on the ASAP7 PDK with over 19.5k cells spanning 149 types using automatic tools, targeting six metrics: signal rise/fall delay, transition, and power. Experimental results demonstrate that FusionCell reduces regression error, with an average MAPE of 0.92 percent, and improves Spearman/Kendall ranking over baselines, while accelerating the characterization process by orders of magnitude compared to circuit simulation.

</details>


### [162] [Plug-and-Play Spiking Operators: Breaking the Nonlinearity Bottleneck in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20289)
*Xinzhe Yuan,Xiang Peng,Bin Gu,Huan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用的框架，通过将Transformer中的非线性运算（除法、指数、L2范数）分解为基于LIF神经元群的脉冲友好型近似，实现无需微调的ANN-to-SNN转换，在LLM上仅产生<1%的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ANN-to-SNN转换方法主要支持线性运算，对Softmax、SiLU、归一化等非线性算子的支持有限，这些非线性操作通常涉及除法、指数、范数计算，与LIF神经元的脉冲动力学不兼容。

Method: 将非线性计算分解为除法、指数、L2范数三种原语，通过LIF神经元群的群体计算实现，结合轻量级位移缩放替代浮点运算，组合成模块化的算子块来支持Softmax、SiLU、归一化等常见非线性函数。

Result: 在多种LLM Transformer上的实验表明，选择性替换目标非线性算子在所有评估任务上的精度下降小于1%。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了SNN转换中非线性算子支持的空缺，无需微调即可实现高精度转换，为脉冲大语言模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical, training-free route to spiking large language models. However, current pipelines primarily focus on spike-driven realizations for Transformer linear-algebra operations, while providing limited support for key nonlinear operators. This gap limits compatibility with neuromorphic-style execution constraints, where such nonlinearities typically require division, exponentiation, or norm computations that are not naturally supported by standard leaky integrate-and-fire dynamics. To solve this problem, we propose a plug-and-play framework that implements spike-friendly approximations for Transformer nonlinearities and integrates into existing ANN-to-SNN pipelines. Our method decomposes these nonlinear computations into three recurring primitives -- division, exponentiation, and $\ell_2$ norms -- and realizes them via population computation using LIF neuron groups, combined with lightweight bit-shift scaling to avoid floating-point arithmetic. By composing these primitives as modular operator blocks, our framework supports common Transformer nonlinearities (e.g., Softmax, SiLU, and normalization) without any fine-tuning. Experiments on a range of LLMs Transformers show that selectively replacing the targeted nonlinear operators incurs less than a $1\%$ accuracy drop across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [163] [Weasel: Out-of-Domain Generalization for Web Agents via Importance-Diversity Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20291)
*Fatemeh Pesaran zadeh,Seyeon Choi,Xing Han Lù,Siva Reddy,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Weasel是一种用于离线训练Web智能体的轨迹选择方法，通过优化平衡单步重要性和多样性的目标函数，结合目标中心AXTree剪枝和风格一致的推理链，在降低训练成本的同时提升跨域泛化性能，实现约9.7-12.5倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调后的Web智能体面临两个问题：1) 在特定轨迹和领域上微调的智能体难以泛化到域外场景；2) 离线训练因噪声、冗余轨迹和长AXTree状态而计算效率低下。Weasel旨在同时解决泛化性和训练效率问题。

Method: 1) 轨迹选择：通过优化平衡一元重要性和成对多样性（状态、网站、交互模式）的目标函数，用贪心算法选择固定预算的子集；2) 目标中心AXTree剪枝：仅保留地面真值动作目标周围的内容；3) 风格匹配：用模型生成且风格一致的推理链替代专家轨迹。

Result: 在AgentTrek和NNetNav训练集上，使用Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma3-4B和Qwen3-8B模型在WebArena、WorkArena、MiniWob上评估，Weasel提升了域外性能，同时减少训练成本，实现约9.7-12.5倍训练加速。

Conclusion: Weasel通过智能轨迹选择和AXTree剪枝，有效提升了Web智能体的跨域泛化能力和训练效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled web agents that follow natural language goals through multi-step browser interactions. However, agents fine-tuned on specific trajectories and domain often struggle to generalize out of domain, and offline training can be compute-inefficient due to noisy, redundant trajectories and long accessibility-tree (AXTree) states. To address both issues, we propose Weasel, a trajectory selection method for offline training of web agents. Weasel selects a fixed-budget subset of trajectory steps by optimizing an objective that balances unary importance with pairwise diversity over states, websites, and interaction patterns, solving efficiently with a greedy algorithm. We further improve efficiency with target-centered AXTree pruning that keeps only content around the ground-truth action target, and we mitigate style mismatch for reasoning-native models by replacing expert traces with model-generated, style-consistent rationales. Across AgentTrek and NNetNav training datasets, evaluations in WebArena, WorkArena, and MiniWob, and experiments with Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma3-4B, and Qwen3-8B, Weasel improves out-of-domain performance while reducing training cost, producing roughly 9.7-12.5$\times$ training speedups over standard fine-tuning. We make the code available at https://github.com/fatemehpesaran310/weasel.

</details>


### [164] [TreeText-CTS: Compact, Source-Traceable Tree-Path Evidence for Irregular Clinical Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20292)
*Kwanhyung Lee,Juhwan Choi,Jongheon Kim,Joohyung Lee,Hyeongwon Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeText-CTS是一种将不规则EHR时间序列转换为可读、紧凑、可溯源树路径证据单元的方法，在多个临床预测任务中优于现有基于文本的接口，且性能与数值模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数值时间序列模型能有效处理不规则EHR数据，但无法提供可读的风险估计依据；基于文本的接口要么序列化冗长，要么摘要难以溯源。需要一种兼具可读性、紧凑性和可追溯性的方法。

Method: 将不规则EHR轨迹通过XGBoost模型路由多尺度窗口摘要，将激活的树路径口头化为确定性阈值条件组成的证据单元；通过证据选择器筛选信息子集，再由语言模型编码器整合进行预测。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012死亡率预测、MIMIC-III死亡率预测和PhysioNet 2019脓毒症预测中，TreeText-CTS在所有基于文本的接口中取得最佳AUROC和AUPRC，AUPRC比最强基线提升6.0-9.7个百分点，且与数值模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 树路径证据构建、证据选择和语言模型组合三个模块均对性能有贡献；由于每个传递给语言模型编码器的片段都由激活的树路径阈值条件构成，最终预测器的证据可被检查和溯源。

Abstract: Numerical time-series models can effectively process irregular electronic health record (EHR) trajectories, but they do not naturally expose the measurements and temporal patterns supporting each risk estimate as readable evidence. Existing text-based interfaces improve readability, but typically rely on either raw serialization, which is lengthy and redundant, or patient-level free-form summaries, which are difficult to trace to source measurements and time windows. To bridge this gap, we introduce TreeText-CTS (Clinical Time-Series), which converts irregular EHR trajectories into human-readable, compact, source-traceable tree-path evidence units without patient-level summarization or inference-time autoregressive decoding. TreeText-CTS routes multi-scale window summaries through frozen XGBoost models and verbalizes activated tree paths as deterministic, source-traceable evidence units composed of threshold conditions. An evidence selector assembles an informative subset of these units, which a language-model encoder then integrates for prediction. Across PhysioNet 2012 mortality, MIMIC-III mortality, and PhysioNet 2019 sepsis-onset forecasting, TreeText-CTS achieves the best AUROC and AUPRC among evaluated text-based EHR time-series interfaces, improving AUPRC by 6.0 to 9.7 absolute percentage points over the strongest prior text-based interface while remaining competitive with numerical time-series models. Ablations show that tree-path evidence construction, evidence selection, and language-model composition each contribute to performance. Because every span passed to the language-model encoder is constructed from activated tree-path threshold conditions, TreeText-CTS makes the evidence supplied to the final predictor inspectable and source-traceable.

</details>


### [165] [Quant.npu: Enabling Efficient Mobile NPU Inference for on-device LLMs via Fully Static Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20295)
*Jinghe Zhang,Daliang Xu,Chenghua Wang,Weikai Xie,Tao Qi,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Quant.npu，一种专为移动设备NPU设计的全静态量化框架，通过可学习量化参数、旋转矩阵和两阶段优化策略，在满足NPU硬件约束的同时实现高效低比特量化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法依赖动态激活量化，与移动设备NPU要求的全静态量化不兼容，导致LLM在NPU上推理效率受限。

Method: 提出Quant.npu框架，包含：(1) 可学习量化参数与旋转矩阵实现整数-only全静态量化；(2) 旋转与比特宽度感知的初始化策略；(3) 分布感知的两阶段选择性优化流程；(4) 敏感性引导的自适应混合精度方案。

Result: 在真实移动NPU上达到与SOTA方法相当的精度，同时推理延迟降低最高15.1%。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的初始化与选择性优化策略，成功将高保真PTQ适配到NPU硬件约束下，实现了精度与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, where Neural Processing Units (NPUs) necessitate fully static quantization for optimal inference efficiency. However, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods predominantly rely on dynamic activation quantization, rendering them incompatible with NPU hardware constraints. To bridge the gap between high-fidelity PTQ and NPU-constrained inference, we propose Quant.npu, a integer-only fully static quantization framework. It incorporates learnable quantization parameters and rotation matrices, enabling low-bit activation-weight quantization without runtime quantization parameters re-computation. Crucially, we identify that initialization and selective optimization of quantization parameters is pivotal for optimization stability, as improper initialization and naive joint optimization induce gradient instability that disrupts the optimization of rotation matrices. To address this, we propose a rotation-and-bit-width-aware initialization tailored to diverse activation profiles and a distribution-aware selective optimization (two-stage quantization pipeline) tailored to rotated and unrotated tensors. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision scheme to balance accuracy with inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on real-world mobile NPUs demonstrate that Quant.npu achieves comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 15.1%.

</details>


### [166] [Spectral Unforgetting: Post-Hoc Recovery of Damaged Capabilities Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20296)
*Aarash Abro,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-Hard方法，通过对微调权重更新Δ进行Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值处理，在保留目标任务收益的同时修复被破坏的通用能力，并在14个设置和9个跨域基准上取得最佳平衡修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调语言模型会破坏其原本具备的通用能力（灾难性遗忘），现有方法要么需要数据依赖的调参，要么简单回退到基座模型。本文希望在不依赖数据的前提下，区分微调更新中的"有用信号"和"有害噪声"，实现精准修复。

Method: 将微调更新Δ=W_ft-W_base视为低秩任务对齐信号与类IID噪声残差的叠加，对每个权重差值矩阵应用Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值（SVD滤波），保留高能量结构化部分，移除谱体（spectral bulk）。仅需基座和微调两个检查点，无需数据。

Result: 在14个（模型，任务）设置和9个跨域基准上，DG-Hard在事后修复基线中取得最强的平衡修复效果；同时在不使用任何对齐数据的情况下，恢复了良性微调所破坏的安全对齐能力。

Conclusion: 微调导致的部分能力损失并非专业化的必然代价，而是权重更新中可移除的谱残差。这为理解灾难性遗忘的机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model for a target task routinely degrades capabilities the training data never explicitly threatened. We study this phenomenon, known as catastrophic forgetting, and propose a post-hoc repair solution that uses only the pretrained checkpoint $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ and its fine-tuned descendant $W_{\mathrm{ft}}$. The goal is not merely to revert the model toward the base checkpoint, but to recover capabilities damaged by fine-tuning while preserving both the target-task gains and any beneficial held-out improvements. We introduce DG-Hard, a checkpoint-only spectral repair method for the fine-tuning update $Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$. DG-Hard treats $Δ$ as a low-rank task-aligned signal embedded in an IID-like noise residual that gradient descent has no incentive to remove, and applies the Donoho-Gavish hard singular-value threshold to each weight-delta matrix, keeping the structured high-energy part of the update and removing the spectral bulk. This reduces repair to a closed-form SVD filtering step requiring no data-dependent tuning. A central difficulty is evaluation: average accuracy hides per-benchmark failures, while naive recovery scores reward models that simply revert toward the base. We therefore introduce a partition-conditional metric that separately tracks healing, preservation, non-damage, and target-task retention. Across $14$ (model, task) settings and nine cross-domain held-out benchmarks, DG-Hard achieves the strongest balanced repair among post-hoc baselines. DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

</details>


### [167] [Mechanisms of Misgeneralization in Physical Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20299)
*Kento Nishi,Raphael Tang,Karun Kumar,Core Francisco Park,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式序列模型在物理领域规划运动时，虽然每个单独轨迹看似合理，但聚合后的物理量分布会偏离训练数据的意图，这种现象被称为"物理误泛化"。研究通过合成任务揭示了该现象源于模型局部误差通过物理测量传播，并提出了数据偏差核来预测和缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学和机械模拟等物理领域，工程师精心策划演示数据以控制物理量（如运动距离、机械能）的分布，但发现标准深度学习模型生成的轨迹在聚合分布层面违背了设计意图，从而引发了对这一现象的机理探究需求。

Method: 通过可控合成任务建立物理误泛化的机制解释；利用数据偏差核（data deviation kernel）估计模型局部误差，预测物理量的质量增减；在迷宫导航和双摆运动等任务上验证预测有效性；基于机制提出核信息干预策略。

Result: 证明物理误泛化源于模型类固有的局部误差通过物理测量函数传播，导致恢复的分布发生偏移；数据偏差核能够成功预测合成任务及应用任务中哪些物理量会增益或损失质量。

Conclusion: 揭示了物理误泛化的机制，为识别有前景的缓解策略提供了结构性指导，并提出了基于核信息的干预方法。

Abstract: Generative sequence models are often trained to plan motion in physical domains, from robotics to mechanical simulations. When constructing a dataset to train such a model, engineers may curate demonstrations to specify how trajectories should be distributed over a physical quantity like travel distance or mechanical energy. For example, a roboticist building a maze navigation agent might choose demonstrations whose travel distances cover a fixed range uniformly, hoping to constrain the agent's expected power usage. We find that standard deep learning can violate this intent: each generated trajectory can seem plausible on its own, but the aggregate distribution over the physical quantity is wrong. We call this failure physical misgeneralization, and develop an account of its mechanism. Using controlled synthetic tasks, we show that physical misgeneralization arises when local errors typical of the model class propagate through the physical measurement to shift the recovered distribution. We estimate these errors with a data deviation kernel, and we use it to predict which physical quantities gain or lose mass in both our synthetic and more applied maze navigation and double-pendulum motion tasks. Finally, our mechanistic interpretation helps identify which mitigation strategies are structurally promising, and we use it to propose a kernel-informed intervention.

</details>


### [168] [Robust Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Models for Low-Dimensional Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20300)
*Zheng Zhai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒的子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），用于从高维数据中学习低维结构。该模型在SQMF框架下支持多种噪声分布（如广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯），通过梯度算法和回溯线搜索策略实现高效优化。实验表明，该方法在鲁棒性和重建精度上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间约束二次矩阵分解（SQMF）框架在处理非高斯噪声（如重尾噪声）时鲁棒性不足，亟需一种能够适应多种噪声分布的通用模型。

Method: 基于SQMF框架，将噪声建模推广至广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯分布；设计带回溯线搜索的梯度下降算法求解非凸优化问题；对ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数进行敏感性分析。

Result: 在重尾和轻尾噪声条件下，所提模型均表现出可靠性能；数值实验验证了其在鲁棒性和重建精度上的一致优势。

Conclusion: SCQM模型通过泛化噪声分布假设，有效提升了低维结构学习对多样化噪声环境的鲁棒性，为高维数据分析提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust subspace-constrained quadratic model (SCQM) for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data. Building upon the subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization (SQMF) framework, the proposed model accommodates a broad class of noise distributions, including generalized Gaussian and radial Laplace models. This generalization enables reliable performance under both heavy-tailed and light-tailed noise, thereby substantially enhancing robustness across diverse data regimes. To efficiently address the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a gradient-based algorithm equipped with a backtracking line-search strategy that ensures stable and efficient convergence. In addition, we present a sensitivity analysis of the $\ell_p^p$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions, elucidating their distinct behaviors under varying noise characteristics. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods in terms of robustness and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [169] [Neural Collapse by Design: Learning Class Prototypes on the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20302)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Theodoros Giannakopoulos,Mihalis A. Nicolaou,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现交叉熵和对比学习本质上都是超球面上的原型对比方法，分别从不同路径提出了改进方案（NTCE/NONL和固定原型SCL），使监督学习在超球面上达到神经坍缩（NC）理论最优状态，大幅提升了准确率、收敛速度和迁移学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督分类的两大范式——交叉熵（CE）和监督对比学习（SCL）在实际中都未能达到理论最优的神经坍缩（NC）状态：CE留下径向自由度未约束，收敛到退化几何结构；SCL在预训练中驱动特征趋向NC，但在后续的线性探测阶段丢弃了这一结构。作者希望弥合这两条路径与理论最优之间的差距。

Method: 提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失函数，从CE侧引入对比学习的关键要素（大有效负样本集、解耦的对齐与均匀性项）；从SCL侧证明其目标已隐式优化了一个原理性分类器（权重为类均值嵌入），线性探测既冗余又有害，因此采用固定原型替代。

Result: 在包括ImageNet-1K在内的四个基准上，NTCE和NONL超越CE准确率，逼近NC（≥95%），在4/5指标上以不到CE 7.5%的迭代次数达到CE的收敛NC水平；SCL固定原型方法可匹配线性探测而无需数小时的分类器训练。迁移学习提升5.5%，严重类别不平衡下提升8.7%，ImageNet-C上mCE更低。

Conclusion: 监督学习本质上是超球面上的原型学习，通过两条路径设计均可达到神经坍缩理论最优，重新定义了监督分类的范式。

Abstract: Supervised classification has a theoretical optimum, Neural Collapse (NC), yet neither of its two dominant paradigms reaches it in practice. Cross entropy (CE) leaves radial degrees of freedom unconstrained and converges to a degenerate geometry, while supervised contrastive learning (SCL) drives features toward NC during pretraining but discards this structure in a post hoc linear probing phase. We show that both paradigms are different appearances of the same method, prototype contrast on the unit hypersphere, and that closing the gap requires fixing each at its specific point of failure. From the CE side, we propose NTCE and NONL, two normalized losses that import contrastive optimization's missing ingredients into classifier learning: a large effective negative set and decoupled alignment and uniformity terms. From the SCL side, we prove that SCL's objective already optimizes throughout training for a principled classifier whose weights are the class mean embeddings, making linear probing both redundant and harmful. Empirically, on four benchmarks including ImageNet-1K, NTCE and NONL surpass CE accuracy, closely approximate NC ($\geq 95\%$), and match CE's converged NC on 4/5 metrics in under $7.5\%$ of its iterations, while SCL with fixed prototypes matches linear probing without the hours-long classifier training phase. The learned geometry yields $+5.5\%$ mean relative improvement in transfer learning, up to $+8.7\%$ under severe class imbalance, and lower mCE on ImageNet-C, recasting supervised learning as prototype learning on the hypersphere, with NC reached by design on both paths.

</details>


### [170] [AirfoilGen: A valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20303)
*Zhijie Yang,Min Tang,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AirfoilGen，一种基于潜扩散模型的翼型生成方法，通过圆扫表示法保证几何有效性，并利用条件扩散模型实现对气动性能（升力/阻力系数）的精确控制，在超20万翼型数据集上训练，性能调控精度达98.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成方法在翼型设计中存在几何无效性高、对气动性能缺乏有效控制的问题，亟需一种既能保证几何合法性又能精确调控气动性能的生成方法。

Method: 提出圆扫表示法约束生成过程保证几何有效性；利用Transformer编码翼型形状为潜向量，再以条件扩散模型根据目标气动性能进行去噪生成；构建超20万翼型的大规模数据集。

Result: AirfoilGen在几何有效性和气动性能可控性上显著优于现有方法，平均性能调控精度达到98.41%。

Conclusion: AirfoilGen验证了通过结合几何约束表示与条件潜扩散模型，可以在翼型生成中同时实现高几何合法性和精确的气动性能控制。

Abstract: Airfoil shape design is a fundamental task in aerospace engineering, with a direct impact on flight stability and fuel consumption. Deep learning has recently emerged as a promising tool for this task, but existing deep generative approaches remain limited in both geometric validity and physical controllability. They offer little control over the generated shapes, yielding invalid geometries, and they typically do not condition effectively on aerodynamic performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes AirfoilGen, a valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil. It first introduces a novel airfoil representation scheme, the circle sweeping representation, to constrain the generative process so that output shapes respect essential airfoil characteristics. It then enables explicit control over aerodynamic performance (e.g., lift and drag coefficients) by operating in a learned latent space: a transformer model encodes airfoil shapes into vector embeddings, and a conditional diffusion model denoises Gaussian noise into these latent embeddings while incorporating target aerodynamic performance. In addition, this paper presents a new dataset of over 200,000 airfoils, which is substantially larger than the widely used UIUC airfoil dataset (1,650 airfoils) and more suitable for training modern deep generative models. Experiments demonstrate that AirfoilGen enables airfoil generation with far greater geometric validity and aerodynamic performance controllability than previously achievable, with an average performance-conditioning accuracy of 98.41%.

</details>


### [171] [Less Data, Faster Training: repeating smaller datasets speeds up learning via sampling biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20314)
*Jingwen Liu,Ezra Edelman,Surbhi Goel,Bingbin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了"小样本多次重复训练相比大样本单次训练可能更节省计算资源"的现象，并发现这一现象源于采样偏差带来的逐层增长优势，尤其适用于推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到在算法任务、多种架构和优化器下，使用较少样本但重复多次训练比使用更大数据集更节省计算，而现有理论无法解释这一现象，因此希望揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 通过理论分析和多种干预实验，研究了层状增长（layer-wise growth）在采样偏差下的表现，对比不同数据集大小和重复次数对训练效率的影响。

Result: 发现小数据集多次重复能够带来计算节省，其关键在于采样偏差促进了逐层增长的优化过程，这种效应在大数据集下不明显。

Conclusion: 小样本重复训练不仅是在数据稀缺时的权宜之计，还可以主动利用为一种有利的归纳偏置，特别是在推理任务中。

Abstract: This work investigates the ``small-vs-large gap'', where repeating on fewer samples can lead to compute saving during training compared to using a larger dataset. This is observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures and optimizers and cannot be explained using prior theory. We argue that the speedup comes from appropriate layer-wise growth enabled by sampling biases, which is more pronounced when the dataset size is smaller. We provide both theoretical analysis and empirical evidence from various interventions. Our results suggest that using a smaller dataset with more repetitions is not just a fallback strategy under data scarcity, but can be proactively leveraged as a favorable inductive biases for optimization, particularly in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [172] [Causal Unlearning in Collaborative Optimization: Exact and Approximate Influence Reversal under Adversarial Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20341)
*Ali Mahdavi,Azadeh Zamanifar,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Omid Kashefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HF-KCU方法，通过Krylov子空间共轭梯度迭代近似影响函数来高效移除联邦学习中客户端的数据贡献，在保持模型精度的同时实现47.75倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习系统需要支持数据删除请求以符合隐私法规，但从头重新训练计算成本过高。

Method: 通过Krylov子空间中的共轭梯度迭代近似影响函数移除客户端贡献，复杂度从O(d³)降至O(kd)；采用因果加权机制确保仅更新持有已删除数据的客户端；设计应对海森矩阵和梯度有界对抗扰动的能力。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（Dirichlet α=0.5分区）上比重新训练快47.75倍，测试精度与基线相差仅0.60%（71.16% vs 71.76%）；成员推理攻击成功率为0.499，与重训练模型一致。

Conclusion: HF-KCU在效率和精度上适合生产级联邦系统，能处理异步到达的删除请求，提供可解释性和收敛保证。

Abstract: Federated learning systems must support data deletion requests to comply with privacy regulations, yet retraining from scratch after each deletion is computationally prohibitive. We present HF-KCU, a method that removes a client's contribution by approximating the influence function through conjugate gradient iterations in Krylov subspaces, reducing complexity from O(d^3) to O(kd) where k<<d.A causal weighting mechanism ensures that only clients holding the deleted data receive parameter updates, preventing spurious changes to unaffected clients. Our method is designed to handle bounded adversarial perturbations to the Hessian and gradient, providing graceful degradation under realistic threat models. We validate HF-KCU across convolutional (ResNet-18, SimpleCNN) and transformer (ViT-Lite) architectures on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and Fashion-MNIST. On CIFAR-10 under Dirichlet (alpha=0.5) partitioning, HF-KCU achieves 47.75 times speedup over retraining while maintaining test accuracy within 0.60% of the rational baseline(71.16 vs 71.76 %). Membership inference attacks on the forget set yield success rates of 0.499 matching the retrained model and confirming effective privacy restoration. We provide convergence guarantees showing that the Krylov approximation error decreases as O((k ^1/2-1)/(k^1/2+1)) where k is the Hessian condition number. The causal weighting mechanism ensures surgical updates, where only clients holding deleted data are modified, preserving model quality for unaffected participants and avoiding the instability of gradient-based approaches in asynchronous federated settings. This design provides interpretability as each update is directly traceable to the influence of the deleted data. The method's efficiency and precision make it suitable for production federated systems where deletion requests arrive asynchronously and computational budgets are constrained.

</details>


### [173] [Symmetrization of Loss Functions for Robust Training of Neural Networks in the Presence of Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20347)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种通过对称化方法构建鲁棒损失函数的理论框架，提出了多类别unhinged loss，并证明其是唯一凸的多类别对称损失函数。在此基础上引入了SGCE和alpha-MAE两种插值损失函数，在噪声标签基准测试中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注训练集成本高且易出错，设计对标签噪声鲁棒的损失函数是一个重要问题。对称性条件为鲁棒性提供了理论保证，本文旨在通过对称化方法研究多类别损失函数的鲁棒性。

Method: 利用多类别损失函数的唯一分解（对称分量+类别不敏感项）进行对称化，推导出多类别unhinged loss；证明了其在凸损失中的唯一性及局部线性近似性质；进一步提出SGCE和alpha-MAE两种插值损失函数，以在unhinged loss和MAE之间平滑过渡并控制beta-光滑性。

Result: 证明了多类别unhinged loss是唯一满足对称性条件的凸多类别损失函数；证明了任何对称损失在等分量分数向量附近的线性近似等价于多类别unhinged loss；SGCE和alpha-MAE在标准噪声标签基准测试中与现有鲁棒损失函数相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 对称化方法为设计鲁棒损失函数提供了理论基础，多类别unhinged loss在凸对称损失中占据核心地位，插值损失函数在实际应用中表现出色。

Abstract: Labeling a training set is often expensive and susceptible to errors, making the design of robust loss functions for label noise an important problem. The symmetry condition provides theoretical guarantees for robustness to such noise. In this work, we study a symmetrization method arising from the unique decomposition of any multi-class loss function into a symmetric component and a class-insensitive term. In particular, symmetrizing the cross-entropy loss leads to a linear multi-class extension of the unhinged loss. Unlike in the binary case, the multi-class version must have specific coefficients in order to satisfy the symmetry condition. Under suitable assumptions, we show that this multi-class unhinged loss is the unique convex multi-class symmetric loss. We also show that it has a fundamental local role: the linear approximation of any symmetric loss around score vectors with equal components is equivalent to the multi-class unhinged loss. We then introduce SGCE and alpha-MAE, two loss functions that interpolate between the multi-class unhinged loss and the Mean Absolute Error while allowing control of the beta-smoothness of the loss. Experiments on standard noisy-label benchmarks show competitive performance compared with existing robust loss functions.

</details>


### [174] [Nonlocal operator learning for fMRI encoding and decoding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20389)
*Andreas Kramer,Saugat Acharya,Alice Giola,Emanuele Zappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经积分算子的框架用于fMRI编解码任务，系统研究了时空上下文窗口大小对性能的影响，发现更长的时域窗口能提升预测效果并产生更结构化的潜在表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据具有高维时空结构，传统方法难以有效建模其中的非局部时空依赖关系，亟需设计专门捕获这类依赖的模型架构。

Method: 构建潜在神经积分算子框架，在辅助空间中进行不动点迭代，通过解码器实现分类和刺激预测；系统对比不同时空窗口（短/长时间窗口、视觉皮层/全脑记录）的影响。

Result: 更长的时域窗口在多项任务中均提升性能；解码实验中潜在空间比原始数据具有更清晰的类别分离；编码实验虽整体性能适中，但长窗口仍有一致增益。

Conclusion: 神经积分算子是建模fMRI动态的有前景框架，更广泛的时空上下文对预测和表征学习均有裨益，捕获分布式非局部脑动态需要专门设计的模型架构。

Abstract: Functional MRI data exhibit high-dimensional spatiotemporal structure, making both prediction and decoding challenging. In this work, we investigate neural integral-operator-based models for encoding and decoding tasks in fMRI, with particular emphasis on the role of nonlocal spatiotemporal context. We implement a latent neural integral operator framework that performs fixed point iterations in an auxiliary space from which classification and stimuli prediction is performed via a decoder. We evaluate our model on two open-source fMRI datasets.
  Our experiments examine both decoding of stimuli from fMRI recordings and encoding of fMRI dynamics from stimulus representations. A main focus is the effect of spatiotemporal context: we systematically compare short and long temporal windows, as well as the use of visual cortex vs whole brain recordings, and analyze their influence on performance and latent-space geometry. Across tasks and datasets, larger temporal windows generally improve results and produce more structured learned representations. In decoding experiments, the learned latent space often provides clearer class separation than the raw data. In encoding experiments, although absolute performance remains moderate due to the difficulty of the task, longer temporal windows still yield consistent gains.
  These findings suggest that neural integral operators provide a promising framework for modeling fMRI dynamics and that broader spatiotemporal context can be beneficial for both prediction and representation learning. More broadly, the results indicate that exploiting distributed nonlocal structure in brain dynamics requires model architectures specifically designed to capture such dependencies.

</details>


### [175] [Score-Based Causal Discovery of Latent Variable Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20396)
*Ignavier Ng,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Biwei Huang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评分的方法，用于识别包含因果相关潜变量的因果结构，并提供了可识别性保证。通过设计合理的评分函数，实现了潜变量因果模型的评分等价性和一致性，同时开发了精确和连续的评分方法，统一了多种现有基于约束的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于约束的方法（如条件独立性检验、秩亏检验）存在测试顺序依赖、误差传播、显著性水平选择等经验性问题，而评分方法（如GES）在无潜变量场景下表现良好，但在潜变量场景下极具挑战。因此，本文旨在开发一种能有效识别包含潜变量的因果结构的评分方法。

Method: 论文首先证明了一种合理设计的评分函数能够实现潜变量因果模型的评分等价性和一致性；其次，对可观测变量边际下的自由度进行了刻画，考虑了多种结构假设；最后，基于此开发了精确和连续的评分方法，统一了多种现有的基于约束的方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法在识别包含潜变量的因果结构方面是有效的，验证了评分方法在潜变量场景下的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将评分方法扩展到潜变量因果模型的结构学习中，提供了可识别性理论保证，并统一了多种现有方法的视角，为潜变量因果发现提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: Identifying latent variables and the causal structure involving them is essential across various scientific fields. While many existing works fall under the category of constraint-based methods (with e.g. conditional independence or rank deficiency tests), they may face empirical challenges such as testing-order dependency, error propagation, and choosing an appropriate significance level. These issues can potentially be mitigated by properly designed score-based methods, such as Greedy Equivalence Search (GES) (Chickering, 2002) in the specific setting without latent variables. Yet, formulating score-based methods with latent variables is highly challenging. In this work, we develop score-based methods that are capable of identifying causal structures containing causally-related latent variables with identifiability guarantees. Specifically, we show that a properly formulated scoring function can achieve score equivalence and consistency for structure learning of latent variable causal models. We further provide a characterization of the degrees of freedom for the marginal over the observed variables under multiple structural assumptions considered in the literature, and accordingly develop both exact and continuous score-based methods. This offers a unified view of several existing constraint-based methods with different structural assumptions. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [176] [Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402)
*Xiaocan Li,Shiliang Wu,Zheng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MXFP4量化误差的三路分解理论，将误差分为尺度偏置、死区截断和网格噪声三个分量，并针对每个分量设计靶向修正方法，在RL后训练中恢复BF16精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将MXFP4量化误差视为单一噪声项，忽视了不同误差成分对RL训练的不同破坏机制，导致精度损失严重。

Method: 理论证明MXFP4量化误差可分解为三个加性分量：尺度偏置（幂次舍入）、死区截断（小值归零）和网格噪声（最近邻4bit舍入）。针对各分量设计修正：宏块缩放、异常值回退和自适应量化噪声（AQN）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B稠密模型和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base混合专家模型上，靶向修正分别将BF16精度恢复到0.7%和3.0%以内。

Conclusion: 通过将量化误差分解并针对性修正，MXFP4可在RL后训练中大幅降低精度损失，验证了误差解耦方法的有效性。

Abstract: MXFP4 arithmetic can dramatically accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), yet the quantization error introduces severe accuracy degradation. Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical and empirical analysis decomposes the MXFP4 quantization error into three additive components: "scale bias" from power-of-two rounding, "deadzone truncation" from zeroing small values, and "grid noise" from rounding to the nearest 4-bit grid. Each component dominates a distinct RL failure mode: scale bias accumulates multiplicatively through the backward pass, affecting gradient accuracy; deadzone truncation degrades rollout quality; and grid noise raises the policy's entropy. We combine corrections that are RL failure mode-targeted but not component-exclusive: Macro-block scaling to reduce scale bias, Outlier Fallback recovers deadzone entries, but also partially reduces scale bias induced error, and Adaptive Quantization Noise (AQN) for controlling the policy entropy. On Qwen2.5-3B dense and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base mixture-of-experts model, the targeted corrections recover BF16 accuracy to within 0.7% and 3.0% respectively.

</details>


### [177] [Spectral Souping: A Unified Framework for Online Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20408)
*Yinlam Chow,Guy Tennenholtz,Ted Yun,James Harrison,Arthur Gretton,Andre Barreto,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Souping框架，通过在LLM中发现通用谱表示并利用模型合并技术，先离线学习多维度偏好策略，再在线高效融合，实现无需重训即可动态适配个体用户偏好，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF方法只能对齐聚合偏好，难以处理用户之间多样且冲突的个性化需求。

Method: 提出Spectral Souping框架：1）发现LLM中通用的谱表示，证明其适合模型合并；2）离线学习一组针对不同偏好维度的专业策略；3）在线推理时通过合并输出或参数高效融合这些策略。

Result: 在在线偏好对齐基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种可扩展且计算高效的方案，使LLM能够动态适应个体用户偏好，无需昂贵的在线重训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) effectively aligns Large Language Models (LLMs) with aggregate human preferences but often fails to address the diverse and conflicting needs of individual users. To overcome this issue, we introduce Spectral Souping, a unified framework for efficient, online preference alignment. Our contribution is the discovery of a universal spectral representation within LLMs, which is proven to be highly amenable to model merging. This theoretical insight enables a two-phase methodology: we first learn a basis of specialized policies offline, each focused on a distinct, fine-grained preference dimension. An online adaptation algorithm then efficiently ``soups'' these policies at inference time, either by merging their outputs or parameters, enabling rapid model adaptation without the need for costly online retraining w.r.t. tailored preference rewards. Experiments on online preference alignment benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance improvements over existing state-of-the-art approaches, presenting a scalable and computationally efficient solution for dynamically adapting LLMs to individual user preferences.

</details>


### [178] [Supervised Latent Restructuring for Small-Data Quantum Learning in Plant Phenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20413)
*Alakananda Mitra,David H. Fleisher,Vangimalla Reddy,Chittaranjan Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维小样本生物学数据的分类问题，提出了一种结合PCA压缩、LDA监督重构和GPU加速量子核对齐(QKA)的混合工作流。实验表明监督重构提升了几何可分性，但经典分类器表现不一，且在有限优化预算下QKA仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据中特征维度与样本量严重不匹配，传统核方法在极端小样本场景下因潜在压缩丢失类别区分结构而表现不佳，亟需探索新的表征几何设计来提升分类性能。

Method: 将1280维深度图像嵌入压缩至64维PCA空间，再通过LDA重构为11维监督潜在空间，最后在NVIDIA L40S硬件上执行GPU加速的量子核对齐(QKA)分类。

Result: 监督重构使Silhouette系数从原始空间的0.003和PCA-64的-0.006提升至LDA-11的0.197。线性SVM和XGBoost在重构空间中表现提升，而RBF-SVM和随机森林性能下降。QKA在该压缩维度下性能有限。

Conclusion: 表征几何是小样本量子学习的关键设计变量，但仅靠潜在空间几何不足以实现强可训练的量子性能，从压缩表征中恢复非线性判别结构仍存在实际困难。

Abstract: High-dimensional biological data often exhibit a severe mismatch between feature dimensionality and sample size, making reliable classification difficult in extremely small-data regimes. In these settings, kernel methods can lose discriminative power when latent compression fails to preserve class-separating structure. We study this problem in fine-grained plant phenomics and propose a hybrid workflow that compresses 1280-dimensional deep image embeddings into a 64-dimensional PCA space and then restructures them into an 11-dimensional supervised latent space using Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA), followed by GPU-accelerated Quantum Kernel Alignment (QKA) on NVIDIA L40S hardware. Empirically, supervised latent restructuring substantially improves the geometric separability of the compressed representation, increasing the Silhouette coefficient from 0.003 in the raw embedding space and -0.006 in PCA-64 to 0.197 in the supervised LDA-11 space. However, downstream classical evaluation reveals a clear compression trade-off: Linear SVM and XGBoost improve in the restructured latent space, whereas RBF-SVM and Random Forest degrade under the same 11-dimensional bottleneck. Under a constrained optimization budget, QKA in this regime remains challenging, indicating that latent geometry alone is not sufficient for strong trainable quantum performance. These findings position representation geometry as a central design variable in small-data quantum learning and expose the practical difficulty of recovering nonlinear discriminative structure from aggressively compressed biological representations.

</details>


### [179] [Miller-Index-Based Latent Crystallographic Fracture Plane Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20416)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能否利用晶面指数（Miller指数）作为结构化潜在表征来推理断裂几何，实验表明MLLMs能在理想化设置下进行可靠的潜在推理，并能主动拒绝不适用物理场景的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MLLMs是否能够利用Miller指数这种结构化潜在表征进行断裂几何的物理感知推理，并评估模型识别表征适用边界的能力。

Method: 将Miller指数z=(h,k,l)形式化为控制理想化平面断裂的潜在变量，设计了两种能力评估：潜在推理（视觉观测映射到平面假设）和潜在适用性评估（判断表征对断裂图像是否有意义）。实验涵盖合成数据、受控2D-3D几何配对以及陶瓷、玻璃、金属、混凝土等多类材料的真实断裂图像。

Result: MLLMs在理想化设置下能可靠地进行潜在推理，并且能够主动拒绝在底层物理不支持的情况下的潜在表征。

Conclusion: MLLMs可以作为具有物理感知能力的推理系统，在有明确领域有效性建模的条件下，能够利用结构化潜在先验进行推理。

Abstract: We study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can leverage crystallographic plane indices (Miller indices) as a structured latent representation for reasoning about fracture geometry. We formulate Miller indices $z = (h,k,l)$ as a latent variable governing idealized planar fracture and evaluate two complementary capabilities: (i) latent inference, where the model maps visual observations to plane hypotheses under physically valid conditions, and (ii) latent applicability assessment, where the model determines whether such a representation is meaningful for a given fracture image.
  Through extensive experiments spanning synthetic data, controlled 2D--3D geometric pairs, and real-world fracture images across multiple material classes -- including ceramics, glass, metals, and concrete -- we show that MLLMs can reliably perform latent inference in idealized settings and, critically, can reject the latent representation when the underlying physics does not support it. These results suggest that MLLMs can act as physics-aware reasoning systems conditioned on structured latent priors, provided that the domain of validity is explicitly modeled.

</details>


### [180] [Can Conversational XAI Improve User Performance? An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20439)
*Sven Kruschel,Julian Rosenberger,Lasse Bohlen,Mathias Kraus,Patrick Zschech*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种评估对话式XAI助手效果的实验设计，初步结果显示两种辅助方式都能帮助用户超越模型，但两者间无显著差异，且用户参与度不高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI技术虽旨在增强用户表现，但效果有限；对话式XAI助手有潜力突破这一局限，但其对客观绩效的实证证据不足。

Method: 采用可解释性设计预测模型，创建用户可通过识别和补偿系统错误来超越模型的条件；对比对话式辅助与问答式辅助的效果；通过预测准确性、模型理解和错误识别三个指标评估。

Result: 42名参与者在两种辅助条件下均显著优于模型表现，但两种辅助类型之间没有显著差异，且整体用户参与度较低。

Conclusion: 初步结果为后续完整研究提供了改进方向，包括增强用户参与度的干预措施，以及探索提升预测表现的内在机制。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) techniques aim to provide insights into predictive models and enhance user performance, yet they often fall short of these expectations. Conversational XAI assistants promise to overcome such limitations, but empirical evidence on their impact on objective performance measures remains limited. We propose an experimental design for evaluating explanation assistance through prediction accuracy, model understanding, and error identification. Using an explainable-by-design prediction model, we create conditions where users can outperform the model by identifying and compensating for systematic errors. We compare conversational assistance against Q&A-based assistance to assess which better supports users in working with model explanations. Preliminary results from testing our experimental design show that participants (N=42) in both treatments significantly outperformed the model but reveal no performance differences between assistance types and modest engagement overall. These findings inform refinements for our planned full study, including enhanced engagement interventions and investigation of the mechanisms driving improved predictions.

</details>


### [181] [Weight Decay Regimes in Grokking Transformers: Cheap Online Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20441)
*Lucky Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现权重衰减（weight decay）是Transformer在模算术任务中从记忆→泛化→崩溃状态转换的控制参数，并提出了两种基于注意力激活的低成本在线诊断指标（平均成对注意力头余弦相似度和熵标准差）。通过多尺度实验确定了记忆-泛化边界的临界值λ_c=0.0158，经验幂律指数ν=0.757，并在多种架构（MLP、LSTM、Mamba）中验证了该控制模式的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer在模算术任务中的学习状态转换机制，寻找低成本、易获取的诊断指标来追踪训练动态，并系统研究权重衰减作为控制参数的作用。

Method: 在11种实验条件、3种模型规模（0.82M-85M参数）下训练Transformer，以权重衰减为控制变量；提出两种基于注意力激活的诊断指标（平均余弦相似度、熵标准差）；采用logistic拟合定位临界点，用幂律拟合计算经验指数；进行多任务复制、注意力头重初始化、权重范数裁剪等消融实验；在MLP、LSTM、Mamba架构上进行跨架构验证。

Result: 权重衰减轴清晰区分了记忆、发展型grokking和崩溃三个区域；临界点λ_c=0.0158（95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200]）；经验幂律指数ν=0.757（CI [0.725, 0.799]），排除了ν=1/2和3D Ising ν≈0.63；注意力头重初始化显著改变相位2振幅（Cohen's d=-1.190），权重范数裁剪无此效果；跨架构实验复现了权重衰减控制模式但架构特定λ_c不同。

Conclusion: 权重衰减是Transformer模算术学习状态转换的有效控制参数；提出的注意力激活诊断指标成本低且有效；记忆-泛化边界存在明确的临界值；ν=0.757是经验值，普适性分类需要更密集的有限尺寸标度研究；该发现范围限定于小规模Transformer注意力模型上的模算术任务。

Abstract: Transformers trained on modular arithmetic exhibit sharp transitions between memorization, generalization, and collapse. We show that weight decay acts as a scalar empirical control parameter for these regimes, and introduce two cheap online diagnostics, mean pairwise attention-head cosine similarity and entropy standard deviation, that track training dynamics from attention activations alone and complement loss-landscape diagnostics at lower compute cost. Across eleven experimental conditions and three model scales (0.82M to 85M parameters), the weight-decay axis separates memorization, developmental grokking, and collapse. A near-transition logistic fit localizes the memorization-to-developmental boundary at $λ_c=0.0158$ (95% CI [0.0109, 0.0200], N=210); a power-law fit gives an empirical exponent $ν=0.757$ (CI [0.725, 0.799]). Reference exponents $ν=1/2$ and 3D Ising $ν\approx 0.63$ lie outside this empirical CI under our four-bin grid, so we report $ν$ as empirical and defer universality-class identification to denser finite-size-scaling work. A horizon-matched multi-task replication (n=280, four modular operations) preserves the weight-decay control pattern; a paired attention-head re-initialization experiment at $λ=0.05$ changes Phase-2 amplitude (Cohen's $d=-1.190$, n=10, $p_t=4.5 \times 10^{-3}$), while matched weight-norm clipping does not. Three cross-architecture probes (4L MLP, 4L LSTM, and 4L Mamba; each n=70) replicate the weight-decay-controlled transition with architecture-specific $λ_c$ values.
